  3  | export class SearchResultsPage {
  4  |   readonly page: Page;
  5  |   readonly results: Locator;

  11 | 
  12 |   constructor(page: Page) {
  13 |     this.page = page;
  14 |     this.results = page.locator('[data-component-type="s-search-result"]');
  15 |     this.firstResult = page.locator('[data-component-type="s-search-result"]').first();
  16 |     this.minPriceInput = page.locator("#low-price, input[aria-label='Minimum price']").first();
  17 |     this.maxPriceInput = page.locator("#high-price, input[aria-label='Maximum price']").first();
  18 |     this.priceSubmit = page.locator("#go-button, .a-button-text[aria-label='Go - Submit price range']").first();
  19 |     this.sortDropdown = page.locator("#s-result-sort-select");
  20 |   }
  21 | 
  22 |   async dismissLocationPopupIfPresent() {
  23 |     try {
  24 |       for (const text of ["Dismiss", "CONTINUAR"]) {
  25 |         const btn = this.page.locator(`text="${text}"`).first();
  26 |         if (await btn.isVisible({ timeout: 2000 })) {
  27 |           await btn.click();
  28 |           await this.page.waitForTimeout(800);
  29 |           break;
  30 |         }
  31 |       }
  32 |     } catch {
  33 |       // No popup, continue
  34 |     }
  35 |   }
  36 | 
  37 |   async waitForResults() {
  38 |     await this.dismissLocationPopupIfPresent();
  39 |     await this.results.first().waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 15000 });
  40 |   }
  41 | 
  42 |   async getResultCount(): Promise<number> {
  43 |     return await this.results.count();
  44 |   }
  45 | 
  46 |   async filterByPriceRange(min: string, max: string) {

  48 |       await this.dismissLocationPopupIfPresent();
  49 |       await this.minPriceInput.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 8000 });
  50 |       await this.minPriceInput.fill(min);
  51 |       await this.maxPriceInput.fill(max);
  52 |       await this.priceSubmit.click();

> 55 |       throw new Error("Price filter inputs not found — Amazon may have changed the UI for this region.");
